<script type="application/ld+json">{"@context":"https:\/\/schema.org","@type":"NewsArticle","headline":"Partililer \"de\u011fi\u015fim\" dedi! \u00d6n se\u00e7imde zafer Selda Sezgin'in","articleSection":"Siyaset","dateCreated":"2026-10-18T19:59:00+03:00","datePublished":"2026-10-18T19:59:00+03:00","dateModified":"2026-10-18T20:13:32+03:00","wordCount":360,"genre":"news","mainEntityOfPage":{"@type":"WebPage","@id":"https:\/\/www.haberlisin.com\/partililer-degisim-dedi-on-secimde-zafer-selda-sezginin"},"articleBody":"\r\nSEZG\u0130N\u2019E 3 B\u0130N 886 OY\r\nYeni Parti\u2019nin Sakarya te\u015fkilat\u0131nda il ba\u015fkanl\u0131\u011f\u0131 i\u00e7in yap\u0131lan \u00f6n se\u00e7im tamamland\u0131. Sakarya\u2019n\u0131n 16 il\u00e7esinde kurulan sand\u0131klarda oy kullanan \u00fcyeler, Selda Sezgin ve Ecevit Kele\u015f aras\u0131nda tercih yapt\u0131. 2 aday\u0131n yar\u0131\u015ft\u0131\u011f\u0131 \u00f6n se\u00e7imde Selda Sezgin 3 bin 886 oyla ilk s\u0131rada yer ald\u0131. Ecevit Kele\u015f ise 3 bin 359 oy ald\u0131. A\u00e7\u0131klanan sonu\u00e7lara g\u00f6re 2 aday\u0131n toplam oyu 7 bin 245 olurken, Sezgin\u2019in ald\u0131\u011f\u0131 oylar\u0131n bu toplam i\u00e7indeki pay\u0131 yakla\u015f\u0131k y\u00fczde 53,6 oldu.\r\n\r\n\r\n\r\n16 \u0130L\u00c7EDE SANDIK KURULDU\r\n\r\n\u00d6n se\u00e7im kapsam\u0131nda Sakarya genelindeki 16 il\u00e7ede e\u015f zamanl\u0131 oy verme i\u015flemi ger\u00e7ekle\u015ftirildi. Sabah saatlerinde ba\u015flayan oylamada parti \u00fcyeleri, il ba\u015fkanl\u0131\u011f\u0131 s\u00fcrecine ili\u015fkin tercihlerini sand\u0131\u011fa yans\u0131tt\u0131.\r\n\r\n\r\n\r\nSEZG\u0130N: \u201cBU B\u0130R BAYRAK YARI\u015eIDIR\u201d\r\n\r\n\u00d6n se\u00e7imi kazanan Selda Sezgin, sonu\u00e7lar\u0131n ard\u0131ndan birlik mesaj\u0131 vererek hedeflerinin iktidar oldu\u011funu s\u00f6yledi. Sezgin, \u201cHep s\u00f6yledi\u011fimiz gibi bu i\u015fin kazanan\u0131, kaybedeni yok. Bu i\u015fin kazanan\u0131 hep Yeni Parti olacak. Bu bir bayrak yar\u0131\u015f\u0131d\u0131r. Biz de bu bayra\u011f\u0131 en tepeye, en zirveye \u00e7\u0131karmak i\u00e7in elimizden gelen ne varsa yapaca\u011f\u0131z. Hedefimiz her zaman iktidar olacak\u201d dedi.\r\n\r\n\r\n\r\nKELE\u015e\u2019TEN SEZG\u0130N\u2019E TEBR\u0130K VE B\u0130RL\u0130K MESAJI\r\n\r\nEcevit Kele\u015f de Sezgin\u2019i tebrik ederek parti i\u00e7in birlikte \u00e7al\u0131\u015fmaya devam edeceklerini belirtti. Date();return[d.getFullYear(),String(d.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,"0"),String(d.getDate()).padStart(2,"0")].join("-");}function excluded(){const path=location.pathname.toLowerCase();return CONFIG.excludedPaths.some(item=>path.startsWith(item));}function canShow(){if(CONFIG.testMode)return true;const <script>
(function() {
    function initWeatherSelect() {
        $(document).off('change', '.weather-city-select').on('change', '.weather-city-select', function() {
            var $select = $(this);
            var citySlug = $select.val();

            if (!citySlug) {
                return;
            }

            // Weather widget container'ını bul
            var $weatherWidget = $select.closest('.weather-widget, .weather-top');
            if (!$weatherWidget.length) {
                $weatherWidget = $select.parents().has('.degree, img.condition').first();
            }
            if (!$weatherWidget.length) {
                $weatherWidget = $select.closest('.weather').parent().find('.weather-widget, .weather-top').first();
            }
            if (!$weatherWidget.length) {
                $weatherWidget = $('.weather-widget').first();
            }

            // Route URL'ini al
            var routesUrl = (typeof Service !== 'undefined' && Service.routes && Service.routes.url) 
                ? Service.routes.url 
                : '/service';

            var isDistrict = true;

            var requestUrl = routesUrl + (isDistrict ? '/weather-district' : '/weather');

            var requestData = {
                ref_lang: (typeof TE !== 'undefined' && TE.setting && TE.setting.language) ? TE.setting.language : null,
            };

            if (isDistrict) {
                var selectedOption = $select.find('option:selected');
                var districtSlug = citySlug;
                var parentCitySlug = selectedOption.data('city') || null;
                if (!parentCitySlug) {
                    // city slug yoksa, district olarak tek parametre gönder
                    requestData.city = districtSlug;
                } else {
                    requestData.city = parentCitySlug;
                    requestData.district = districtSlug;
                }
            } else {
                requestData.city = citySlug;
            }

            // Loading göster
            if ($weatherWidget.length) {
                $weatherWidget.find('.widget-loading').removeClass('d-none').addClass('d-flex');
            }
            
            // AJAX isteği
            $.ajax({
                type: 'GET',
                url: requestUrl,
                data: requestData,
                cache: true,
                dataType: 'json',
                success: function(result) {
                    if (!result.error && result.data) {
                        if ($weatherWidget.length) {
                            $weatherWidget.find('img.condition').attr('src', result.data.image);
                            $weatherWidget.find('.degree').text(result.data.degree);
                            var $desc = $weatherWidget.find('.description');
                            if ($desc.length) {
                                $desc.text(result.data.desc);
                            }
                            $weatherWidget.find('.widget-loading').removeClass('d-flex').addClass('d-none');
                            $weatherWidget.find('.row').removeClass('d-none');
                        } else {
                            $('img.condition').attr('src', result.data.image);
                            $('.degree').text(result.data.degree);
                        }
                    } else {
                        console.log(result.message || 'Weather data not found');
                        if ($weatherWidget.length) {
                            $weatherWidget.find('.widget-loading').removeClass('d-flex').addClass('d-none');
                        }
                    }
                },
                error: function(xhr, status, error) {
                    console.error('Weather request error:', error);
                    if ($weatherWidget.length) {
                        $weatherWidget.find('.widget-loading').removeClass('d-flex').addClass('d-none');
                    }
                }
            });
        });
    }
    
    if (typeof jQuery !== 'undefined') {
        jQuery(document).ready(initWeatherSelect);
    } else {
        var checkJQuery = setInterval(function() {
            if (typeof jQuery !== 'undefined') {
                clearInterval(checkJQuery);
                jQuery(document).ready(initWeatherSelect);
            }
        }, 100);
    }
})();
</script>

<script>
(function() {
    'use strict';

    function initHeader13Categories() {
        var swiperContainer = document.querySelector('.header-13 .header-13-categories-swiper');
        if (!swiperContainer) return;

        var categoriesSwiper = new Swiper('.header-13 .header-13-categories-swiper', {
            slidesPerView: 'auto',
            spaceBetween: 0,
            loop: false,
            freeMode: {
                enabled: true,
                sticky: false
            },
            mousewheel: {
                forceToAxis: true
            },
            grabCursor: true,
            navigation: {
                nextEl: '.header-13 .header-13-swiper-button-next',
                prevEl: '.header-13 .header-13-swiper-button-prev'
            },
            on: {
                init: function() {
                    updateNavigationButtons(this);
                },
                resize: function() {
                    this.update();
                    updateNavigationButtons(this);
                },
                setTranslate: function() {
                    updateNavigationButtons(this);
                }
            }
        });

        function updateNavigationButtons(swiper) {
            var prevButton = document.querySelector('.header-13 .header-13-swiper-button-prev');
            var nextButton = document.querySelector('.header-13 .header-13-swiper-button-next');
            if (!prevButton || !nextButton) return;

            var totalWidth = 0;
            swiper.slides.forEach(function(slide) {
                if (!slide.classList.contains('swiper-slide-duplicate')) {
                    totalWidth += slide.offsetWidth;
                }
            });

            var containerWidth = swiper.el.offsetWidth;

            if (totalWidth <= containerWidth) {
                prevButton.classList.remove('swiper-button-active');
                nextButton.classList.remove('swiper-button-active');
            } else {
                if (swiper.isBeginning) {
                    prevButton.classList.remove('swiper-button-active');
                } else {
                    prevButton.classList.add('swiper-button-active');
                }
                if (swiper.isEnd) {
                    nextButton.classList.remove('swiper-button-active');
                } else {
                    nextButton.classList.add('swiper-button-active');
                }
            }
        }

        var resizeTimer;
        window.addEventListener('resize', function() {
            clearTimeout(resizeTimer);
            resizeTimer = setTimeout(function() {
                categoriesSwiper.update();
                updateNavigationButtons(categoriesSwiper);
            }, 250);
        });
    }

    if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initHeader13Categories);
    } else {
        initHeader13Categories();
    }
})();
</script>

<script>
    var shareableModelId = 28357153;
    var shareableModelClass = 'TE\\Blog\\Models\\Post';

    function shareCount(id, model, platform, url) {
        fetch("https://www.haberlisin.com/sharecount", {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: {
                'Content-Type': 'application/json',
                'X-CSRF-TOKEN': document.querySelector('meta[name="csrf-token"]')?.getAttribute('content')
            },
            body: JSON.stringify({ id, model, platform, url })
        }).catch(err => console.error('Share count fetch error:', err));

        bumpShareCounter();
    }

    // Paylaşım sayacını yerelde +1 göster. Kalıcı değer edge /v1/hit yanıtından
    // gelir ama o taban 15dk cache'li — kullanıcı kendi paylaşımının sayıya
    // yansıdığını hemen görsün.
    //
    // Sayaç tire (—) ise, yani değer 0 ya da henüz gelmemişse, ilk paylaşım
    // onu "1" yapar. statsRaw dataset'ten okunur (tire metninden sayı
    // üretilemez); yoksa 0 varsayılır.
    function bumpShareCounter() {
        const box = document.querySelector('[data-stats="share"]');
        if (!box) {
            return;
        }
        const target = box.querySelector('[data-stats-value="share"]');
        if (!target) {
            return;
        }
        const current = parseInt(box.dataset.statsRaw || '0', 10) || 0;
        const next = current + 1;
        box.dataset.statsRaw = String(next);
        target.textContent = window.HitManagement
            ? new window.HitManagement().formatShort(next)
            : String(next);
    }

    function goSharePopup(url, title, width = 600, height = 400) {
        const left = (screen.width - width) / 2;
        const top = (screen.height - height) / 2;
        window.open(
            url,
            title,
            `width=${width},height=${height},left=${left},top=${top},resizable=yes,scrollbars=yes`
        );
    }

    async function handleMobileShare(event, title, url) {
        event.preventDefault();

        if (shareableModelId && shareableModelClass) {
            shareCount(shareableModelId, shareableModelClass, 'native_mobile_share', url);
        }

        const isAndroidWebView = navigator.userAgent.includes('Android') && !navigator.share;

        if (isAndroidWebView) {
            window.location.href = 'androidshare://paylas?title=' + encodeURIComponent(title) + '&url=' + encodeURIComponent(url);
            return;
        }

        if (navigator.share) {
            try {
                await navigator.share({ title: title, url: url });
            } catch (error) {
                if (error.name !== 'AbortError') {
                    console.error('Web Share API failed:', error);
                }
            }
        } else {
            alert("Bu cihaz paylaşımı desteklemiyor.");
        }
    }

    function initiateDesktopShare(event, platformOverride = null) {
        event.preventDefault();
        const anchor = event.currentTarget;
        const platform = platformOverride || anchor.dataset.platform;
        const webShareUrl = anchor.href;
        const contentUrl = anchor.dataset.shareUrl || webShareUrl;

        if (shareableModelId && shareableModelClass && platform) {
            shareCount(shareableModelId, shareableModelClass, platform, contentUrl);
        }

        goSharePopup(webShareUrl, platform ? platform.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + platform.slice(1) : "Share");
    }

    function copyURL(event, urlToCopy) {
        event.preventDefault();
        navigator.clipboard.writeText(urlToCopy).then(() => {
            alert('Bağlantı panoya kopyalandı!');
        }).catch(err => {
            console.error('Could not copy text: ', err);
            try {
                const textArea = document.createElement("textarea");
                textArea.value = urlToCopy;
                textArea.style.position = "fixed";
                document.body.appendChild(textArea);
                textArea.focus();
                textArea.select();
                document.execCommand('copy');
                document.body.removeChild(textArea);
                alert('Bağlantı panoya kopyalandı!');
            } catch (fallbackErr) {
                console.error('Fallback copy failed:', fallbackErr);
            }
        });
    }

    function printContent(event) {
        event.preventDefault();

        const triggerElement = event.currentTarget;
        const contextContainer = triggerElement.closest('.infinite-item') || document;

        const header      = contextContainer.querySelector('.post-header');
        const media       = contextContainer.querySelector('.news-section .col-lg-8 .inner, .news-section .col-lg-8 .ratio, .news-section .col-lg-8 iframe');
        const articleBody = contextContainer.querySelector('.article-text');

        if (!header && !media && !articleBody) {
            window.print();
            return;
        }

        let printHtml = '';
        
        if (header) {
            const titleEl = header.querySelector('h1');
            const descEl  = header.querySelector('.description, h2.lead');

            let cleanHeaderHtml = '<div class="printed-header">';
            if (titleEl) cleanHeaderHtml += titleEl.outerHTML;
            if (descEl)  cleanHeaderHtml += descEl.outerHTML;
            cleanHeaderHtml += '</div>';

            printHtml += cleanHeaderHtml;
        }

        if (media) {
            printHtml += media.outerHTML;
        }

        if (articleBody) {
            const articleClone = articleBody.cloneNode(true);
            articleClone.querySelectorAll('.post-flash').forEach(function (el) {
                el.parentNode.removeChild(el);
            });
            printHtml += articleClone.outerHTML;
        }
        const iframe = document.createElement('iframe');
        iframe.style.position = 'fixed';
        iframe.style.right = '0';
        iframe.style.bottom = '0';
        iframe.style.width = '0';
        iframe.style.height = '0';
        iframe.style.border = '0';
        document.body.appendChild(iframe);

        const frameWindow = iframe.contentWindow || iframe;
        const title = document.title || 'Yazdır';

        // Yazdırma belgesi DOM API ile kurulur. Eskiden doc.write ile tam bir HTML
        // belgesi metni yazılıyordu; o metin sayfa kaynağında fazladan head/body
        // etiketleri gibi görünüp SEO denetim araçlarını yanıltıyordu.
        // Yalnız doctype yazılır: standart modda (quirks değil) boş html/head/body oluşur.
        const doc = frameWindow.document;
        doc.open();
        doc.write('<!DOCTYPE html>');
        doc.close();

        doc.documentElement.lang = 'tr';

        const charset = doc.createElement('meta');
        charset.setAttribute('charset', 'utf-8');
        doc.head.appendChild(charset);

        doc.title = title;

        // Sayfanın stilleri kopyalanır; <link>'ler yüklenince yazdırılır (aksi halde çıktı stilsiz kalır)
        const styleLoads = [];
        document.querySelectorAll('link[rel="stylesheet"], style').forEach(function (el) {
            const clone = doc.importNode(el, true);
            if (clone.tagName === 'LINK') {
                styleLoads.push(new Promise(function (resolve) {
                    clone.onload = clone.onerror = resolve;
                }));
            }
            doc.head.appendChild(clone);
        });

        const printStyle = doc.createElement('style');
        printStyle.textContent =
            'html, body { margin: 0; padding: 0; background: #ffffff; }' +
            '.printed-article { margin: 0; padding: 20px; box-shadow: none; background: #ffffff; }';
        doc.head.appendChild(printStyle);

        const wrapper = doc.createElement('div');
        wrapper.className = 'printed-article';
        wrapper.innerHTML = printHtml;
        doc.body.appendChild(wrapper);

        // Stil dosyası takılırsa yazdırma sonsuza dek beklemesin
        const stylesReady = Promise.race([
            Promise.all(styleLoads),
            new Promise(function (resolve) { setTimeout(resolve, 3000); })
        ]);

        stylesReady.then(function () {
            try {
                frameWindow.focus();
                frameWindow.print();
            } finally {
                setTimeout(function () {
                    document.body.removeChild(iframe);
                }, 1000);
            }
        });
    }

    var dropdownButton = document.querySelector('.dropdownButton');
    if (dropdownButton) {
        var icon = dropdownButton.querySelector('#icon');
        var parentDropdown = dropdownButton.closest('.dropdown');
        if (parentDropdown && icon) {
            parentDropdown.addEventListener('show.bs.dropdown', function () {
                icon.classList.remove('fa-plus');
                icon.classList.add('fa-minus');
            });
            parentDropdown.addEventListener('hide.bs.dropdown', function () {
                icon.classList.remove('fa-minus');
                icon.classList.add('fa-plus');
            });
        }
    }
</script>

                            <div class="article-text container-padding" data-text-id="28357153" property="articleBody">
                                <div id="ad_128" data-channel="128" data-advert="temedya" data-rotation="120" class="d-none d-sm-flex flex-column align-items-center justify-content-start text-center mx-auto overflow-hidden mb-3" data-affix="0" data-width="728" data-height="90" style="width: 728px;height: 90px;"></div><div id="ad_128_mobile" data-channel="128" data-advert="temedya" data-rotation="120" class="d-flex d-sm-none flex-column align-items-center justify-content-start text-center mx-auto overflow-hidden mb-3" data-affix="0" data-width="300" data-height="50" style="width: 300px;height: 50px;"></div>
                                <p><br />
<strong>SEZGİN’E 3 BİN 886 OY</strong><br />
Yeni Parti’nin Sakarya teşkilatında il başkanlığı için yapılan ön seçim tamamlandı. Sakarya’nın 16 ilçesinde kurulan sandıklarda oy kullanan üyeler, Selda Sezgin ve Ecevit Keleş arasında tercih yaptı. 2 adayın yarıştığı ön seçimde Selda Sezgin 3 bin 886 oyla ilk sırada yer aldı. Ecevit Keleş ise 3 bin 359 oy aldı. Açıklanan sonuçlara göre 2 adayın toplam oyu 7 bin 245 olurken, Sezgin’in aldığı oyların bu toplam içindeki payı yaklaşık yüzde 53,6 oldu.</p>

<p><img height="900" src="https://haberlisincom.teimg.com/haberlisin-com/uploads/2026/10/yeni-parti-sakarya-il-baskanligi-icin-yapilan-on-secimi-3-bin-886-oy-alan-selda-sezgin-kazandi-1.jpeg" width="1600" /></p>

<p><strong>16 İLÇEDE SANDIK KURULDU</strong></p>

<p>Ön seçim kapsamında Sakarya genelindeki 16 ilçede eş zamanlı oy verme işlemi gerçekleştirildi. Sabah saatlerinde başlayan oylamada parti üyeleri, il başkanlığı sürecine ilişkin tercihlerini sandığa yansıttı.</p>

<p><img height="900" src="https://haberlisincom.teimg.com/haberlisin-com/uploads/2026/10/whatsapp-image-2026-10-18-at-195616.jpeg" width="1600" /></p>

<p><strong>SEZGİN: “BU BİR BAYRAK YARIŞIDIR”</strong></p>

<p>Ön seçimi kazanan Selda Sezgin, sonuçların ardından birlik mesajı vererek hedeflerinin iktidar olduğunu söyledi. Sezgin, “Hep söylediğimiz gibi bu işin kazananı, kaybedeni yok. Bu işin kazananı hep Yeni Parti olacak. Bu bir bayrak yarışıdır. Biz de bu bayrağı en tepeye, en zirveye çıkarmak için elimizden gelen ne varsa yapacağız. Hedefimiz her zaman iktidar olacak” dedi.</p>

<p><img height="900" src="https://haberlisincom.teimg.com/haberlisin-com/uploads/2026/10/whatsapp-image-2026-10-18-at-195620.jpeg" width="1600" /></p>

<p><strong>KELEŞ’TEN SEZGİN’E TEBRİK VE BİRLİK MESAJI</strong></p>

<p>Ecevit Keleş de Sezgin’i tebrik ederek parti için birlikte çalışmaya devam edeceklerini belirtti. Keleş, “9 bin 372 üyenin yaklaşık yüzde 85’inin sandığa yansıdığı ön seçimde yeni başkanımız kardeşim Selda Sezgin olmuştur. Kendisini tebrik ediyorum. Hep beraber, yollara beraber çıkarak, adım adım, kapı kapı dolaşarak, hiç pes etmeden genel başkanımız önderliğinde Yeni Parti’yi Sakarya’da ve Türkiye’de iktidara taşımak için çalışacağız” ifadelerini kullandı.</p>

<p><strong>İL KONGRESİ 25 EKİM’DE</strong></p>

<p>Ön seçimin ardından gözler 25 Ekim Pazar günü yapılacak Yeni Parti Sakarya İl Kongresi’ne çevrildi. Üyelerin tercihinde ilk sırada yer alan Sezgin’in ön seçim zaferinin ardından, il başkanlığına ilişkin süreç kongrede tamamlanacak.</p>

<p></p><div class="article-source py-3 small border-top ">
                        <span class="reporter-name"><strong>Muhabir: </strong>Nazile Akarsu</span>
            </div>

<script data-cfasync="false">
(function() {
    // Bir input'u KENDİ data-challenge'ı ile doldurur. Doldurulmuş input'a dokunmaz.
    function fill(input) {
        if (!input || input.name !== '_js_token') return;
        var challenge = input.getAttribute('data-challenge');
        if (!challenge) return;
        input.value = btoa(JSON.stringify({
            ch: challenge,
            t: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
            r: Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 9)
        }));
    }

    // 1) Hızlı yol: currentScript ile KENDİ input'unu bul, querySelector ile DEĞİL.
    // querySelector('input[name="_js_token"]') sayfadaki İLK input'u alıyordu; footer'da
    // da bu component olduğu için arama/iletişim formundaki input hiç doldurulmuyordu.
    // Rastgele id ÇÖZÜM DEĞİL: md5(uniqid()) HTML'i her render'da değiştirip cache'i kırar.
    var s = document.currentScript;
    var own = s ? s.previousElementSibling : null;
    fill(own);

    // Form submit edilmeden önce token'ı tazele (t/r alanları yenilensin).
    if (own && own.name === '_js_token') {
        var form = own.closest ? own.closest('form') : null;
        if (form) form.addEventListener('submit', function () { fill(own); });
    }

    // 2) EMNİYET AĞI (2026-09-14 — arama beyaz ekran): document.currentScript null
    // olabiliyor (inline script'i taşıyan/erteleyen edge katmanları — ör. Rocket
    // Loader —, eski tarayıcılar, HTML'i yeniden enjekte eden tema script'leri).
    // O durumda token BOŞ gidiyor, ValidateJsToken POST'u reddediyordu.
    // DOM hazır olunca HÂLÂ boş kalan TÜM _js_token input'larını, her biri KENDİ
    // data-challenge'ı ile doldur. Sunucu HTML'i değişmiyor → full-page cache bozulmaz.
    // Component sayfada birden çok kez render olabilir (footer + arama formu).
    // Belge seviyesindeki dinleyiciler SADECE BİR KEZ kurulsun.
    if (window.__teJsTokenGlobal) return;
    window.__teJsTokenGlobal = 1;

    function sweep() {
        var list = document.querySelectorAll('input[name="_js_token"]');
        for (var i = 0; i < list.length; i++) {
            if (!list[i].value) fill(list[i]);
        }
    }

    if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', sweep);
    } else {
        sweep();
    }

    // Submit anında da son bir kez: form'a sonradan eklenen/boş kalan input'u yakala.
    document.addEventListener('submit', function (e) {
        var form = e.target;
        if (!form || !form.querySelectorAll) return;
        var list = form.querySelectorAll('input[name="_js_token"]');
        for (var i = 0; i < list.length; i++) fill(list[i]);
    }, true);
})();
</script>

<script>
// External Link Modal
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', (event) => {
    const siteUrl = TE.setting.url;
    //const allowedDomain = siteUrl.replace(/(^\w+:|^)\/\//, '').replace(/^www\./, '');
    const thisDomain = siteUrl.replace(/(^\w+:|^)\/\//, '').replace(/^www\./, '');


    const allowedDomains = [thisDomain, 'tebilisim.com', 'teimg.com'];


    const modal = document.getElementById("external-link");
    const stayBtn = document.getElementById("stayBtn");
    const continueBtn = document.getElementById("continueBtn");
    let pendingUrl = '';

    $(document).on('click', '.article-text a', function (e) {
         if ($(this).data('template') === 'theme.flow::views.ajax-template.editors') {
            return;
        }
        const url = new URL(this.href);
        const linkHostname = url.hostname.replace(/^www\./, '');


        const isAllowedDomain = allowedDomains.some(domain => {
            return linkHostname === domain || linkHostname.endsWith(`.${domain}`);
        })

        //if (linkHostname !== allowedDomain) {
        if (!isAllowedDomain) {
            e.preventDefault();
            pendingUrl = this.href;
            document.querySelector('.external-url').innerHTML = this.href;
            var myModal = new bootstrap.Modal(document.getElementById('external-link'));
            myModal.show();
        }
    });

    stayBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {

    });

    continueBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
        modal.style.display = "none";
        $('.modal-backdrop').remove();
        window.open(pendingUrl, '_blank');
    });
});
</script>

    // Tables include in table - responsive div

        tables.forEach(table => {
            const div = document.createElement('div');
            div.classList.add('table-responsive');
            table.parentNode.insertBefore(div, table);
            div.appendChild(table);
        });
    });
</script>

    <script>
        (function () {
            function init() {
                if (typeof window.jQuery === 'undefined') { return; }
                var $ = window.jQuery;
                var COOKIE_VALUE    = 1;
                var COOKIE_NAME     = $('div[data-site-cookie-name]').data('site-cookie-name');
                var COOKIE_DOMAIN   = $('div[data-site-cookie-domain]').data('site-cookie-domain');
                var COOKIE_LIFETIME = $('div[data-site-cookie-lifetime]').data('site-cookie-lifetime');
                var SESSION_SECURE  = $('div[data-site-session-secure]').data('site-session-secure') || '';

                function cookieExists(name) {
                    return document.cookie.split('; ').indexOf(name + '=' + COOKIE_VALUE) !== -1;
                }
                function showDialog() {
                    $('.js-cookie-consent').removeClass('is-hidden').each(function () {
                        this.style.setProperty('display', 'block', 'important');
                    });
                }
                function hideDialog() {
                    $('.js-cookie-consent').addClass('is-hidden').each(function () {
                        this.style.setProperty('display', 'none', 'important');
                    });
                }
                function setCookie() {
                    var date = new Date();
                    date.setTime(date.getTime() + (COOKIE_LIFETIME * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000));
                    document.cookie = COOKIE_NAME + '=' + COOKIE_VALUE
                        + ';expires=' + date.toUTCString()
                        + ';domain=' + COOKIE_DOMAIN
                        + ';path=/' + SESSION_SECURE;
                }

                if (cookieExists(COOKIE_NAME)) { hideDialog(); } else { showDialog(); }

                $(document).on('click', '.js-cookie-consent-agree', function () {
                    setCookie();
                    hideDialog();
                });
            }

            if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
                document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', init);
            } else {
                init();
            }
        })();
    </script>

hlArticleTickerMove{from{transform:translate3d(0,0,0)}to{transform:translate3d(-50%,0,0)}}</style></div><div data-pagespeed="true"><script id="haberlisin-mobile-slider-pagination-safe-fix-v2">
(function () {
  "use strict";

  var MOBILE_QUERY = "(max-width: 991.98px)";
  var PAGINATION_SELECTOR = [
    "section.main-headline-11 .swiper.main-headline .swiper-pagination.main-headline-sw",
    "section.main-headline-11 .swiper.main-headline > .d-flex.justify-content-between .swiper-pagination"
  ].join(",");

  function isMobile() {
    return !window.matchMedia || window.matchMedia(MOBILE_QUERY).matches;
  }

  function stopNewsLinkOnly(event) {
    if (!isMobile()) {
      return;
    }

    var target = event.target;
    if (!target || !target.closest) {
      return;
    }

    var bullet = target.closest(".swiper-pagination-bullet");
    if (!bullet) {
      return;
    }

    var pagination = bullet.closest(PAGINATION_SELECTOR);
    if (!pagination) {
      return;
    }

    event.preventDefault();
    event.stopPropagation();
  }

  function bindPaginationFix() {
    var paginations = document.querySelectorAll(PAGINATION_SELECTOR);

    for (var i = 0; i < paginations.length; i += 1) {
      var pagination = paginations[i];

      if (pagination.getAttribute("data-hl-mobile-pagination-safe-fix") === "1") {
        continue;
      }

      pagination.setAttribute("data-hl-mobile-pagination-safe-fix", "1");
      pagination.addEventListener("click", stopNewsLinkOnly, false);
    }
  }

  function boot() {
    bindPaginationFix();
    window.setTimeout(bindPaginationFix, 400);
    window.setTimeout(bindPaginationFix, 1200);
    window.setTimeout(bindPaginationFix, 2500);
  }

  if (document.readyState === "loading") {
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", boot);
  } else {
    boot();
  }

  window.addEventListener("load", bindPaginationFix);
}());
</script>

<script id="haberlisin-mobile-featured-carousel-final">
(function () {
  "use strict";

  var existingController = window.__haberlisinFeaturedCarouselFinal;
  if (existingController && typeof existingController.refresh === "function") {
    existingController.refresh();
    return;
  }

  var MOBILE_QUERY = "(max-width: 991.98px)";
  var REDUCED_MOTION_QUERY = "(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)";
  var SECTION_SELECTOR = "section.main-headline-11";
  var AREA_SELECTOR = SECTION_SELECTOR + " .featured-area-11";
  var CARD_CLASS = "featured-11";
  var TRACK_CLASS = "hl-featured-carousel-final__track";
  var HOST_CLASS = "hl-featured-carousel-final__host";
  var BUTTON_CLASS = "hl-featured-carousel-final__button";
  var PREV_CLASS = "hl-featured-carousel-final__prev";
  var NEXT_CLASS = "hl-featured-carousel-final__next";
  var MOUNT_ATTRIBUTE = "data-hl-featured-carousel-mounted";

  var mobileMedia = window.matchMedia ? window.matchMedia(MOBILE_QUERY) : null;
  var reducedMotionMedia = window.matchMedia ? window.matchMedia(REDUCED_MOTION_QUERY) : null;
  var mountedAreas = new Map();
  var mutationObserver = null;
  var refreshTimer = 0;

  function isMobile() {
    return !mobileMedia || mobileMedia.matches;
  }

  function prefersReducedMotion() {
    return !!(reducedMotionMedia && reducedMotionMedia.matches);
  }

  function getDirectCards(area) {
    var cards = [];

    for (var i = 0; i < area.children.length; i += 1) {
      var child = area.children[i];
      if (child.classList && child.classList.contains(CARD_CLASS)) {
        cards.push(child);
      }
    }

    return cards;
  }

  function createButton(direction) {
    var button = document.createElement("button");
    var isPrevious = direction === "previous";

    button.type = "button";
    button.className = BUTTON_CLASS + " " + (isPrevious ? PREV_CLASS : NEXT_CLASS);
    button.setAttribute("aria-label", isPrevious ? "Önceki öne çıkan haber" : "Sonraki öne çıkan haber");
    button.setAttribute("data-hl-featured-carousel-control", direction);
    button.setAttribute("aria-disabled", "false");

    return button;
  }

  function getActiveIndex(state) {
    var cards = state.cards;
    if (!cards.length) {
      return 0;
    }

    var areaLeft = state.area.getBoundingClientRect().left;
    var activeIndex = 0;
    var smallestDistance = Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY;

    for (var i = 0; i < cards.length; i += 1) {
      var distance = Math.abs(cards[i].getBoundingClientRect().left - areaLeft);

      if (distance < smallestDistance) {
        smallestDistance = distance;
        activeIndex = i;
      }
    }

    return activeIndex;
  }

  function setDisabled(button, disabled) {
    button.disabled = disabled;
    button.setAttribute("aria-disabled", disabled ? "true" : "false");
  }

  function updateState(state) {
    if (!document.documentElement.contains(state.area)) {
      return;
    }

    state.cards = getDirectCards(state.area);

    var controlsHidden = state.cards.length <= 1;
    state.previousButton.hidden = controlsHidden;
    state.nextButton.hidden = controlsHidden;

    if (controlsHidden) {
      setDisabled(state.previousButton, true);
      setDisabled(state.nextButton, true);
      return;
    }

    var activeIndex = getActiveIndex(state);
    state.activeIndex = activeIndex;
    setDisabled(state.previousButton, activeIndex <= 0);
    setDisabled(state.nextButton, activeIndex >= state.cards.length - 1);
  }

  function scheduleStateUpdate(state) {
    if (state.updateFrame) {
      return;
    }

    state.updateFrame = window.requestAnimationFrame(function () {
      state.updateFrame = 0;
      updateState(state);
    });
  }

  function scrollToCard(state, targetIndex, event) {
    event.preventDefault();
    event.stopPropagation();

    updateState(state);

    if (!state.cards.length) {
      return;
    }

    var index = Math.max(0, Math.min(targetIndex, state.cards.length - 1));
    var areaRect = state.area.getBoundingClientRect();
    var cardRect = state.cards[index].getBoundingClientRect();
    var targetLeft = state.area.scrollLeft + cardRect.left - areaRect.left;
    var behavior = prefersReducedMotion() ? "auto" : "smooth";

    if (typeof state.area.scrollTo === "function") {
      state.area.scrollTo({ left: targetLeft, behavior: behavior });
    } else {
      state.area.scrollLeft = targetLeft;
    }

    scheduleStateUpdate(state);
  }

  function removeStaleControls(host) {
    var children = Array.prototype.slice.call(host.children);

    for (var i = 0; i < children.length; i += 1) {
      if (children[i].hasAttribute && children[i].hasAttribute("data-hl-featured-carousel-control")) {
        children[i].remove();
      }
    }
  }

  function mountArea(area) {
    var currentState = mountedAreas.get(area);
    if (currentState) {
      if (currentState.host === area.parentElement) {
        scheduleStateUpdate(currentState);
        return;
      }

      unmountArea(currentState);
    }

    var host = area.parentElement;
    if (!host || !area.closest(SECTION_SELECTOR)) {
      return;
    }

    removeStaleControls(host);

    var previousButton = createButton("previous");
    var nextButton = createButton("next");
    var state = {
      area: area,
      host: host,
      cards: getDirectCards(area),
      previousButton: previousButton,
      nextButton: nextButton,
      activeIndex: 0,
      updateFrame: 0,
      onScroll: null,
      onScrollEnd: null,
      onPreviousClick: null,
      onNextClick: null
    };

    state.onScroll = function () {
      scheduleStateUpdate(state);
    };
    state.onScrollEnd = function () {
      updateState(state);
    };
    state.onPreviousClick = function (event) {
      scrollToCard(state, getActiveIndex(state) - 1, event);
    };
    state.onNextClick = function (event) {
      scrollToCard(state, getActiveIndex(state) + 1, event);
    };

    host.classList.add(HOST_CLASS);
    area.classList.add(TRACK_CLASS);
    area.setAttribute(MOUNT_ATTRIBUTE, "1");
    host.appendChild(previousButton);
    host.appendChild(nextButton);

    area.addEventListener("scroll", state.onScroll, { passive: true });
    if ("onscrollend" in window) {
      area.addEventListener("scrollend", state.onScrollEnd);
    }
    previousButton.addEventListener("click", state.onPreviousClick);
    nextButton.addEventListener("click", state.onNextClick);

    mountedAreas.set(area, state);
    updateState(state);
  }

  function unmountArea(state) {
    if (state.updateFrame) {
      window.cancelAnimationFrame(state.updateFrame);
      state.updateFrame = 0;
    }

    state.area.removeEventListener("scroll", state.onScroll);
    if ("onscrollend" in window) {
      state.area.removeEventListener("scrollend", state.onScrollEnd);
    }
    state.previousButton.removeEventListener("click", state.onPreviousClick);
    state.nextButton.removeEventListener("click", state.onNextClick);

    state.previousButton.remove();
    state.nextButton.remove();
    state.area.scrollLeft = 0;
    state.area.classList.remove(TRACK_CLASS);
    state.area.removeAttribute(MOUNT_ATTRIBUTE);

    if (!state.host.querySelector("." + TRACK_CLASS)) {
      state.host.classList.remove(HOST_CLASS);
    }

    mountedAreas.delete(state.area);
  }

  function refresh() {
    refreshTimer = 0;

    if (!isMobile()) {
      Array.from(mountedAreas.values()).forEach(unmountArea);
      return;
    }

    var areas = Array.prototype.slice.call(document.querySelectorAll(AREA_SELECTOR));
    var activeAreas = new Set(areas);

    areas.forEach(mountArea);

    Array.from(mountedAreas.values()).forEach(function (state) {
      if (!activeAreas.has(state.area) || !document.documentElement.contains(state.area)) {
        unmountArea(state);
      }
    });
  }

  function scheduleRefresh() {
    if (refreshTimer) {
      return;
    }

    refreshTimer = window.setTimeout(refresh, 0);
  }

  function nodeTouchesFeaturedArea(node) {
    if (!node || node.nodeType !== 1) {
      return false;
    }

    if (node.matches(SECTION_SELECTOR) || node.classList.contains("featured-area-11")) {
      return true;
    }

    if (node.querySelector(SECTION_SELECTOR)) {
      return true;
    }

    return node.getElementsByClassName("featured-area-11").length > 0;
  }

  function mutationTouchesFeaturedArea(mutation) {
    if (mutation.type !== "childList") {
      return false;
    }

    if (mutation.target && mutation.target.nodeType === 1 && mutation.target.closest(AREA_SELECTOR)) {
      return true;
    }

    var nodes = Array.prototype.concat.call(
      [],
      Array.prototype.slice.call(mutation.addedNodes),
      Array.prototype.slice.call(mutation.removedNodes)
    );

    return nodes.some(nodeTouchesFeaturedArea);
  }

  function startObserver() {
    if (mutationObserver || !document.body || !window.MutationObserver) {
      return;
    }

    mutationObserver = new MutationObserver(function (mutations) {
      if (mutations.some(mutationTouchesFeaturedArea)) {
        scheduleRefresh();
      }
    });

    mutationObserver.observe(document.body, {
      childList: true,
      subtree: true
    });
  }

  function boot() {
    startObserver();
    scheduleRefresh();
  }

  function destroy() {
    if (refreshTimer) {
      window.clearTimeout(refreshTimer);
      refreshTimer = 0;
    }

    Array.from(mountedAreas.values()).forEach(unmountArea);

    if (mutationObserver) {
      mutationObserver.disconnect();
      mutationObserver = null;
    }

    window.removeEventListener("load", scheduleRefresh);
    window.removeEventListener("resize", scheduleRefresh);
    document.removeEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", boot);

    if (mobileMedia) {
      if (typeof mobileMedia.removeEventListener === "function") {
        mobileMedia.removeEventListener("change", scheduleRefresh);
      } else if (typeof mobileMedia.removeListener === "function") {
        mobileMedia.removeListener(scheduleRefresh);
      }
    }

    delete window.__haberlisinFeaturedCarouselFinal;
  }

  var controller = {
    refresh: scheduleRefresh,
    destroy: destroy
  };

  window.__haberlisinFeaturedCarouselFinal = controller;
  window.addEventListener("load", scheduleRefresh);
  window.addEventListener("resize", scheduleRefresh, { passive: true });

  if (mobileMedia) {
    if (typeof mobileMedia.addEventListener === "function") {
      mobileMedia.addEventListener("change", scheduleRefresh);
    } else if (typeof mobileMedia.addListener === "function") {
      mobileMedia.addListener(scheduleRefresh);
    }
  }

  if (document.readyState === "loading") {
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", boot, { once: true });
  } else {
    boot();
  }
}());
</script>

  var resizeObserver = null;
  var domReadyHandler = null;
  var mediaQuery = typeof window.matchMedia === "function" ? window.matchMedia(MOBILE_QUERY) : { matches: false };

  var api = {
    installed: false,
    reason: document.readyState === "loading" ? "waiting-dom-ready" : "not-installed",
    originalHandlerSource: ORIGINAL_HANDLER_SOURCE,
    originalHandler: null,
    optimizedHandler: null,
    restore: restore
  };
  window[GLOBAL_KEY] = api;

  function sameHeaderSet() {
    var current = document.querySelectorAll("header");
    if (current.length !== cachedHeaders.length) return false;
    for (var index = 0; index < current.length; index += 1) {
      if (current[index] !== cachedHeaders[index]) return false;
    }
    return true;
  }

  function popupIsOpen() {
    return Boolean(document.body && document.body.classList.contains("hpc-lock"));
  }

  function shouldUseOriginal() {
    return popupIsOpen() || !sameHeaderSet();
  }

  function measure() {
    firstHeaderHeight = cachedHeaders.length ? $(cachedHeaders[0]).height() : 0;
    var topHeaders = $(cachedHeaders).find(".top-header");
    topHeaderHeight = topHeaders.length ? topHeaders.first().outerHeight() : 0;
    measurementsDirty = false;
  }

  function setClass(elements, className, enabled) {
    for (var index = 0; index < elements.length; index += 1) {
      var classList = elements[index].classList;
      if (enabled && !classList.contains(className)) classList.add(className);
      if (!enabled && classList.contains(className)) classList.remove(className);
    }
  }

  function setTransform(elements, value) {
    for (var index = 0; index < elements.length; index += 1) {
      if (elements[index].style.transform !== value) elements[index].style.transform = value;
    }
  }

  function applyMobileState() {
    if (measurementsDirty) measure();
    var scrollPosition = window.scrollY;
    var sticky = window.navPosition < scrollPosition;
    var padding = sticky ? firstHeaderHeight + "px" : "0px";
    if (document.body.style.paddingTop !== padding) document.body.style.paddingTop = padding;
    setClass(cachedHeaders, "sticky-header", sticky);

    if (pendingDirection === "up") {
      setClass(cachedHeaders, "slideDown", false);
      setClass(cachedHeaders, "slideUp", true);
      setTransform(cachedHeaders, "translateY(-" + topHeaderHeight + "px)");
    } else if (pendingDirection === "down") {
      setClass(cachedHeaders, "slideUp", false);
      setClass(cachedHeaders, "slideDown", true);
      setTransform(cachedHeaders, "translateY(0)");
    }
    pendingDirection = null;
  }

  function runFrame() {
    rafId = 0;
    if (!api.installed) return;
    applyMobileState();
  }

  function scheduleFrame() {
    if (!rafId) rafId = window.requestAnimationFrame(runFrame);
  }

  function cancelFrame() {
    if (!rafId) return;
    window.cancelAnimationFrame(rafId);
    rafId = 0;
    pendingDirection = null;
  }

  function flushPendingMobileState() {
    if (!rafId) return false;
    window.cancelAnimationFrame(rafId);
    rafId = 0;
    applyMobileState();
    return true;
  }

  function handleScroll() {
    if (!mediaQuery.matches || shouldUseOriginal()) {
      flushPendingMobileState();
      return originalHandler.apply(this, arguments);
    }

    var scrollPosition = window.scrollY;
    if (window.new_scroll_position < scrollPosition && scrollPosition > 80) {
      pendingDirection = "up";
    } else if (window.new_scroll_position > scrollPosition) {
      pendingDirection = "down";
    }
    window.last_scroll_position = scrollPosition;
    window.new_scroll_position = scrollPosition;
    scheduleFrame();
  }

  function invalidateMeasurements() {
    if (!api.installed) {
      measurementsDirty = true;
      return;
    }
    if (!mediaQuery.matches || shouldUseOriginal()) {
      flushPendingMobileState();
      measurementsDirty = true;
      return;
    }
    measurementsDirty = true;
    scheduleFrame();
  }

  function validateHeaders() {
    var headers = Array.prototype.slice.call(document.querySelectorAll("header"));
    var mainHeaders = Array.prototype.slice.call(document.querySelectorAll(".header-13"));
    if (mainHeaders.length !== 1 || headers.indexOf(mainHeaders[0]) === -1) return null;
    for (var index = 0; index < headers.length; index += 1) {
      if (headers[index] !== mainHeaders[0] && !headers[index].closest("#hpc-popup")) return null;
    }
    return headers;
  }

  function findOriginalHandler() {
    if (!$ || typeof $._data !== "function") return null;
    var events = $._data(window, "events");
    var scrollHandlers = events && events.scroll;
    if (!Array.isArray(scrollHandlers)) return null;
    var matches = scrollHandlers.filter(function (entry) {
      return entry && typeof entry.handler === "function" && Function.prototype.toString.call(entry.handler) === ORIGINAL_HANDLER_SOURCE;
    });
    return matches.length === 1 ? matches[0].handler : null;
  }

  function install() {
    if (api.installed || api.reason === "restored") return api.installed;
    if (!window.TE || !window.TE.setting || !(window.TE.setting.fixed_menu > 0)) {
      api.reason = "fixed-menu-disabled";
      return false;
    }
    if (!Number.isFinite(window.navPosition) || !Number.isFinite(window.new_scroll_position)) {
      api.reason = "unsupported-globals";
      return false;
    }
    var headers = validateHeaders();
    if (!headers) {
      api.reason = "unsupported-header-set";
      return false;
    }
    originalHandler = findOriginalHandler();
    if (!originalHandler) {
      api.reason = "unsupported-handler";
      return false;
    }

    cachedHeaders = headers;
    optimizedHandler = handleScroll;
    api.originalHandler = originalHandler;
    api.optimizedHandler = optimizedHandler;
    $(window).off("scroll", originalHandler);
    $(window).on("scroll", optimizedHandler);
    window.addEventListener("resize", invalidateMeasurements, { passive: true });
    if (typeof window.ResizeObserver === "function") {
      resizeObserver = new window.ResizeObserver(invalidateMeasurements);
      for (var index = 0; index < cachedHeaders.length; index += 1) resizeObserver.observe(cachedHeaders[index]);
      for (var headerIndex = 0; headerIndex < cachedHeaders.length; headerIndex += 1) {
        var topHeaders = cachedHeaders[headerIndex].querySelectorAll(".top-header");
        for (var topIndex = 0; topIndex < topHeaders.length; topIndex += 1) resizeObserver.observe(topHeaders[topIndex]);
      }
    }
    measurementsDirty = true;
    api.installed = true;
    api.reason = "installed";
    return true;
  }

  function restore() {
    if (domReadyHandler) {
      document.removeEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", domReadyHandler);
      domReadyHandler = null;
    }
    if (!api.installed) {
      if (api.reason !== "restored") api.reason = "restored";
      return false;
    }
    cancelFrame();
    window.removeEventListener("resize", invalidateMeasurements);
    if (resizeObserver) resizeObserver.disconnect();
    resizeObserver = null;
    $(window).off("scroll", optimizedHandler);
    $(window).on("scroll", originalHandler);
    api.installed = false;
    api.reason = "restored";
    return true;
  }

  if (document.readyState === "loading") {
    domReadyHandler = function () {
      document.removeEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", domReadyHandler);
      domReadyHandler = null;
      install();
    };
    document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded", domReadyHandler, { once: true });
  } else {
    install();
  }
})(window, document);
</script></div><script>
(function () {
    if (/Android|webOS|iPhone|iPad|iPod|BlackBerry|IEMobile|Opera Mini/i.test(navigator.userAgent)) return;

    function isContentLink(a) {
        var href = a.getAttribute('href');
        if (!href) return false;
        if (/^(#|mailto:|tel:|javascript:)/i.test(href)) return false;
        if (a.hasAttribute('download')) return false;
        if (a.getAttribute('target') === '_blank') return false;
        if (a.closest('[data-no-new-tab]')) return false;
        return a.hostname === window.location.hostname;
    }

    function markAll(root) {
        (root || document).querySelectorAll('a[href]').forEach(function (a) {
            if (isContentLink(a)) {
                a.setAttribute('target', '_blank');
                var rel = (a.getAttribute('rel') || '');
                if (rel.indexOf('noopener') === -1) {
                    a.setAttribute('rel', (rel + ' noopener').trim());
                }
            }
        });
    }

    function init() {
        markAll(document);
        // AJAX ile gelen içerik (sonsuz kaydırma, kategori listeleri, swiper klonları)
        new MutationObserver(function (muts) {
            muts.forEach(function (m) {
                m.addedNodes.forEach(function (n) {
                    if (n.nodeType !== 1) return;
                    if (n.tagName === 'A') { markAll(n.parentNode); } else { markAll(n); }
                });
            });
        }).observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true });

        // data-link taşıyan tıklanabilir öğeler (swiper bullet vb.) target attribute almaz
        document.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
            var el = e.target.closest('[data-link]');
            if (!el) return;
            var url = el.getAttribute('data-link');
            if (!url || /^(#|mailto:|tel:|javascript:)/i.test(url)) return;
            e.preventDefault();
            e.stopImmediatePropagation();
            window.open(url, '_blank', 'noopener');
        }, true);
    }

    if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', init);
    } else {
        init();
    }
})();

<script data-cfasync="false">
(function() {
    // Bir input'u KENDİ data-challenge'ı ile doldurur. Doldurulmuş input'a dokunmaz.
    function fill(input) {
        if (!input || input.name !== '_js_token') return;
        var challenge = input.getAttribute('data-challenge');
        if (!challenge) return;
        input.value = btoa(JSON.stringify({
            ch: challenge,
            t: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000),
            r: Math.random().toString(36).substr(2, 9)
        }));
    }

    // 1) Hızlı yol: currentScript ile KENDİ input'unu bul, querySelector ile DEĞİL.
    // querySelector('input[name="_js_token"]') sayfadaki İLK input'u alıyordu; footer'da
    // da bu component olduğu için arama/iletişim formundaki input hiç doldurulmuyordu.
    // Rastgele id ÇÖZÜM DEĞİL: md5(uniqid()) HTML'i her render'da değiştirip cache'i kırar.
    var s = document.currentScript;
    var own = s ? s.previousElementSibling : null;
    fill(own);

    // Form submit edilmeden önce token'ı tazele (t/r alanları yenilensin).
    if (own && own.name === '_js_token') {
        var form = own.closest ? own.closest('form') : null;
        if (form) form.addEventListener('submit', function () { fill(own); });
    }

    // 2) EMNİYET AĞI (2026-09-14 — arama beyaz ekran): document.currentScript null
    // olabiliyor (inline script'i taşıyan/erteleyen edge katmanları — ör. Rocket
    // Loader —, eski tarayıcılar, HTML'i yeniden enjekte eden tema script'leri).
    // O durumda token BOŞ gidiyor, ValidateJsToken POST'u reddediyordu.
    // DOM hazır olunca HÂLÂ boş kalan TÜM _js_token input'larını, her biri KENDİ
    // data-challenge'ı ile doldur. Sunucu HTML'i değişmiyor → full-page cache bozulmaz.
    // Component sayfada birden çok kez render olabilir (footer + arama formu).
    // Belge seviyesindeki dinleyiciler SADECE BİR KEZ kurulsun.
    if (window.__teJsTokenGlobal) return;
    window.__teJsTokenGlobal = 1;

    function sweep() {
        var list = document.querySelectorAll('input[name="_js_token"]');
        for (var i = 0; i < list.length; i++) {
            if (!list[i].value) fill(list[i]);
        }
    }

    if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', sweep);
    } else {
        sweep();
    }

    // Submit anında da son bir kez: form'a sonradan eklenen/boş kalan input'u yakala.
    document.addEventListener('submit', function (e) {
        var form = e.target;
        if (!form || !form.querySelectorAll) return;
        var list = form.querySelectorAll('input[name="_js_token"]');
        for (var i = 0; i < list.length; i++) fill(list[i]);
    }, true);
})();
</script>
